#######################################################################################
# Near-collinear (flux-tube / Wilson-loop OPE) expansion of the six-gluon MHV amplitude
# at order T^2 F^{+-2}, planar N=4 SYM: the two-gluon bound state W_[2] (a = +-2), closed forms.
#
# KINEMATICS (parametrisation of arXiv:1903.10890):
#   u = F/D,  v = F S^2/((1+T^2) D),  w = T^2/(1+T^2),  D = F + F S^2 + S T + F^2 S T + F T^2,
#   T = exp(-tau), S = exp(sigma), F = exp(i phi);  y = S^2;  lny = Log[y] = 2 Log[S].
# COUPLING: g^2 = lambda/(16 pi^2);  loop order L <-> g^(2L)  (1308.2276 uses a = 2 g^2: its a^L coefficient = 2^(-L) x ours).
#
# QUANTITY: the framed hexagon Wilson loop W of 1903.10890 (W = exp[R6 + (Gcusp/4) X] = rho E exp[(Gcusp/4)(X - E1)]).
# OPE: W = 1 + T (F+1/F) W_[1] + T^2 [ (F^2 + 1/F^2) W_[2] + F^0 (...) ] + O(T^3),
#   W_[a] = Int du/(2pi) mu_a(u) T^(gamma_a(u)) S^(i p_a(u))   (single gluon bound state of helicity a, Basso-Sever-Vieira IV, 1407.1736).
# At order T^2 F^{+-2} ONLY W_[2] contributes at LL, NLL and NNLL (k >= L-3):
#   the only other state with F^{+-2} at order T^2 is the two-gluon state W_[1,1] (two gluons of the same helicity),
#   W_[1,1] = (1/2) Int Int mu_1(u) mu_1(v) / (P_{1|1}(u|v) P_{1|1}(v|u)) T^(gamma_1(u)+gamma_1(v)) S^(i p_1(u) + i p_1(v)),
#   which is O(g^8) (mu ~ g^2, 1/P ~ g^2) with no Log[T] at that order, so at L loops it only contributes to Log[T]^k with k <= L-4
#   (N^3LL and beyond); the NNLL blocks below therefore come from W_[2] alone.
# This file: W_[2]^(L) at Log[T]^k = coefficient of g^(2L) T^2 Log[T]^k (F^2 + 1/F^2) in W,
#   W2T2[L,k] = W2T2A[L,k]/y + W2T2B[L,k] + y*W2T2C[L,k],     y = S^2   (only integer powers of S^2 appear),
#   A, B, C pure functions of y built from Iy(a1,...,an) = G(a1,...,an; y) (Goncharov polylogs, indices in {0,-1},
#   Iy(0,..,0) = lny^n/n!; HPL form: Iy(a1..an) = (-1)^(#(-1)) H_{a'}(-y) with -1 -> 1), z_n = zeta(n), of weight <= 2L-1-k
#   (NOT uniform weight: e.g. W2T2[1,0] = ( (1+y^2) Log[1+y] - y^2 lny - y )/(2y) = X|_{T^2 F^2}).
#   Blocks: L = 7, 8, 9 with k = L-3 (NNLL): W2T2[7,4], W2T2[8,5], W2T2[9,6].
#
# CONVERSIONS (exact):
#   Log[W]|_{T^2 F^{+-2}} = W_[2] - (1/2) (lnWT1)^2,   lnWT1 = Log[W]|_{T^1}/(T (F+1/F))  (file hexagon_T1_OPE_LL_NLL.txt),
#   R6|_{T^2 F^{+-2}}     = Log[W]|_{T^2 F^{+-2}} - (Gcusp/4) X|_{T^2 F^{+-2}},   X|_{T^2 F^{+-2}} = ((1+y^2) Log[1+y] - y^2 lny - y)/(2y),
#   E1|_{T^2 F^{+-2}}     = X|_{T^2 F^{+-2}}   (identical), hence with E = exp[R6 + (Gcusp/4) E1]/rho:
#   E|_{T^2 F^{+-2}} = E|_{T^0} * W_[2]   EXACTLY,   E|_{T^0} = rho^(-1) exp[ (Gcusp/4)(-2 Log[T]^2 - lny^2/2 - 2 zeta2) ],
#   (and likewise E|_{T^1} = E|_{T^0} * Log[W]|_{T^1}).  So in the F^{+-1}, F^{+-2} sectors through O(T^2), E/E|_{T^0} = W.
#   Gcusp/4 = g^2 - 2 z2 g^4 + 22 z4 g^6 - (219 z6 + 8 z3^2) g^8 + ... (BES; the code gives it to g^20).
#
# ORIGIN AND VALIDATION:
#   mu_2, gamma_2, p_2 at finite coupling from the BSV flux-tube data by fusion (e^{-t/2} -> e^{-a t/2}, x^{±} -> x(u ± i a/2)),
#   checked against the weak-coupling expansions of 1407.1736 (expansions.txt): E_a and p_a through g^4 and mu_a through NLO
#   for a = 2, 3 agree exactly.  W_[2] evaluated as exact residue sums at z = i u = 1, 2, 3, ... (-> y^1, y^2, ...).
#   Checks: (i) W_[2]^(1) = X|_{T^2 F^2} exactly (R6^(1) = 0);  (ii) the three-loop T^2 F^{+-2} terms of R6 from
#   1308.2276 (ancillary R63_T2.m) are reproduced for ALL Log[T] powers k = 2, 1, 0 through y^10 (k = 0 tests mu_2 at NNLO,
#   beyond the published expansions);  (iii) the closed forms of this file were fitted to long modular series and re-expanded
#   against exact rational series, as described under THIS FILE below.
#######################################################################################
#
# THIS FILE: the NNLL blocks (k = L-3) W2T2[7,4], W2T2[8,5], W2T2[9,6] = W_[2]^(L) at Log[T]^(L-3), fitted mod a 61-bit prime
# from series through y^2990 / y^7900 / y^10150 (lny-triangular solve of 1536 / 4857 / 9735 unknowns), rational reconstruction,
# re-expansion == modular data through the full series length, and == exact rational series through y^40.
# W_[1,1] does not contribute at k = L-3 (it starts at k = L-4), so these are the complete T^2 F^{+-2} NNLL terms of W.
#######################################################################################
